輝達產品時程恐因領導層變動遞延，台股供應鏈重挫
2026 年 11 月 16 日
一、市場動態
1. 輝達執行長閃辭衝擊延燒，台股重挫逾 2500 點：黃仁勳於 11 月 12 日辭去輝達總裁、
執行長及董事職務，由財務長 Colette Kress 暫代執行長，資深董事 Harvey Jones 出
任首任董事長，市場擔憂產品時程與決策速度出現不確定，加上本周財報即將發布，
加深市場觀望情緒。台股今日受上週五美股半導體重挫拖累開低走低，收最低至
41,040 點、下跌 2635 點或 6.03%。台積電跌 135 元或 5.74%，聯發科跌逾 7%，台

達電、創意及台光電、欣興等 PCB／載板族群觸及跌停，高價 AI 股成為賣壓重心。
2. AI 晶片族群同步降溫，資金由「換股」轉為「降低整體曝險」：輝達換帥後，市場原預
期資金將轉進 AMD、博通及 ASIC 等替代方案，惟上週五美國半導體族群全面回檔，
替代受惠股跌幅甚至更大，顯示投資人擔憂的已非單一公司接班問題，而是 AI 投資循
環的整體風險。
二、下跌原因
1. 領導層更替落地，產品時程與決策速度出現不確定：輝達在此事件之前，約 60 名高階
主管直接向黃仁勳匯報，權力高度集中，接班空窗期決策速度恐下降。即使在其任內，
年更節奏已觸及供應鏈物理極限，Rubin CPX 已移出路線圖，Kyber 機櫃傳延至 2028
年。新任管理層若偏重成本與執行穩定，路線圖可能改為「大世代兩年、中間推出強
化版」，800V HVDC、共同封裝光學（CPO）、 M9 等級銅箔基板等升級導入時點恐再
遞延。
2. 規格升級放緩衝擊「本夢比」，高價零組件股首當其衝：台廠 2027 年成長有相當部分
來自 Vera Rubin 單櫃價值約為 GB300 兩倍，而單櫃價值主要來自規格升級。市場擔

憂高階液冷、HVDC 電源與光互連若由 2027 年遞延至 2028 年放量，供應鏈單價、
毛利率與 EPS 成長預估恐面臨下修；缺乏技術突破支撐的高本益比族群評價修正，今
日 PCB／CCL、散熱及電源族群跌停家數最多，即反映此一疑慮。
3. 生態系融資與出口管制成為新增變數：市場估計輝達每季自由現金流約 500 億至 600
億美元，若過渡期暫緩投資及融資交易，生態系資金供給可能暫時減少 500 億美元以
上；據報 Kress 曾反對為 OpenAI 提供最高 1,050 億美元擔保，新增融資審核恐趨嚴，
依賴輝達融資的 NeoCloud 及其供應商受影響較大。此外，事件涉及晶片流向中國之
指控，美方據報擬將訂定較嚴格出口限制，市場估潛在曝險約占輝達營收一成。
4. 期中選舉後，可能的出口管制收緊以及短線獲利了結需求：此事件涉及晶片流向中國之
指控，出口管制收緊將衝擊整體 AI 晶片；台股上週反彈近 4,000 點，短線獲利了結需
求升高，避險心態未退，放大 AI 權值股賣壓。

三、市場看法
1. 2027 年出貨大致鎖定，衝擊先落在評價而非營收：Vera Rubin 已於 8 月量產出貨、
2027 年放量；輝達對供應商之採購與產能承諾達 2,790 億美元，台積電、HBM 及主
要組裝廠 2027 年產能多已預訂。廣達法說表示迄今未見客戶調整訂單，鴻海亦預期
明年 AI 機櫃出貨高雙位數成長。依 ToAlpha 基本情境推算，輝達 CoWoS 份額相對
原本路徑 2027、2028 年僅分別減少 2 與 4 個百分點，CoWoS 總量僅減約 0.6%與

0.8%，影響主要在 2028 年後的產品節奏。
表一：輝達份額變動對 AI 平台與台股供應鏈之影響（情境推估）
項目（相對原本路徑） 2027 年 2028 年
輝達占 CoWoS 需求份額 −2 個百分點 −4 個百分點
輝達平台出貨量 −4.4% −8.9%
AMD 與 ASIC 平台出貨量 +2.5% +5.8%
CoWoS 總量（台積電、封測） −0.6% −0.8%
以輝達平台為主之台股 AI 營收 −3.4% −6.7%
GPU 與 ASIC 兩邊都有之台股 AI 營收 −1.3% −2.3%
以 ASIC 為主之台股 AI 營收 +1.5% +3.6%
資料來源：Bloomberg，元大銀行整理；此為 Bloomberg 情境推估之 AI 營收影響，非盈餘預估
2. 台股供應鏈由分化轉為齊跌，ASIC 族群抗跌性消失：依曝險分組，10 月 29 日至 11
月 12 日以輝達平台為主者平均下跌 14.0%，GPU 與 ASIC 兩邊都有者下跌 17.0%，以
ASIC 為主者下跌 9.4%，同期加權指數下跌 7.1%。兩邊都有之族群多為前波漲幅大的

規格升級零組件股，跌幅反而最深，顯示市場賣的是「評價」而非「訂單」；今日台股
盤勢延續上周五費半情緒，以 ASIC 為主及兩邊都有之代表股平均重挫逾 8%，以輝達
平台為主者跌約 4.4%，顯示賣壓轉向前波漲多、評價較高的 ASIC 與規格升級股，市
場已由「區分受益者」轉為「全面降低 AI 曝險」，短線錯殺機會浮現。

3. 算力多元化早於本次事件，Google TPU 與 AMD 最直接受惠：根據彭博在本次事件發
生前的統計，市場預估輝達占全球 CoWoS 需求比重將由 2026 年 56%降至 2027 年
45%，AMD 由 9%升至 20%，聯發科由 3%升至 7%，預計本次事件發生後，此趨勢
可能進一步加劇。由於 AMD、博通與雲端業者自研 ASIC 同樣高度依賴台積電先進製
程及台廠組裝與零組件，訂單多在台灣供應鏈內部移轉，台灣在 AMD、Google 供應
鏈同樣扮演重要角色。
表三：全球 CoWoS 需求之客戶比例
年度 CoWoS 需求（千片） 輝達 AMD 博通（Google TPU 等） 聯發科
2024 372 54% 11% 18% —
2025 689 62% 9% 12% —
2026（估） 1,394 56% 9% 22% 3%

2027（估） 2,694 45% 20% 18% 7%
資料來源：元大銀行整理，Bloomberg；2026、2027 年為估計值
4. 歷史經驗顯示 CEO 更替後股價無一致方向，基本面才是中期關鍵：回顧美股大型科技
公司交棒案例，計畫性交棒如 Apple、Microsoft 後 12 個月股價分別上漲 77.1%與
20.1%，但同為規劃性交棒的 Amazon 卻下跌 35.3%；突發撤換的 Intel 兩案，3 個月
內均下跌逾 9%，12 個月後則轉為正報酬。惟過去歷史經驗僅能作為參考，輝達後續
走勢仍取決於事件發展與基本面變化而定。

5. 本週三大事件為短線關鍵驗證點：（一）輝達將於美東 11 月 17 日（台灣時間 11 月 18
日凌晨）公布第三季財報，為首度由暫代執行長主持之法說會，焦點在 2028 會計年度
約七成成長指引是否重申、Vera Rubin 放量節奏及新增融資態度；（二）Anthropic 預
計 11 月 18 日定價上市，為檢驗 AI 市場熱度之指標；（三）黃仁勳預計感恩節前赴國
會作證，牽動晶片出口管制法案走向。若財報與指引穩健，市場有望由情緒面回歸基本

面。
四、投資建議
1. 科技股短期審慎、中期仍正向，AI 供應鏈基本面持續具支撐：黃仁勳辭任使輝達面臨
管理層接班、企業治理及政策調查等不確定性，加上 Fed 升息預期及 AI 資料中心監
管疑慮，市場短線波動可能延續。然而，該事件並未直接改變 AI 算力需求，且上週
CoreWeave Q3 財報營收年增 168%、台積電 10 月營收年增 59.7%，均顯示 AI 基礎
建設投資仍具動能。整體對科技股維持短期審慎、但中長期基本面仍在，台灣及日本
等具技術競爭優勢、獲利與現金流穩健的半導體及 AI 供應鏈亦值得留意。惟市場焦點
已由資本支出規模轉向投資報酬與現金流，後續仍須關注輝達財報及管理團隊能否維
持客戶信心。
2. 以股債平衡穿越波動，善用多元資產降低單一事件風險：台股權重高度集中於台積電
與 AI 供應鏈，對單一事件敏感度高。建議將集中度過高的台股科技部位，適度轉向全
球型或多重資產商品，搭配中天期美國公債及高信評投資等級債穩定收益；待接班人
選、財報指引與出口管制方向明朗後，再逐步提高風險資產比重。

NVIDIA Corp. (NVDA-US)  
財報 FIRST TAKE — FY3Q27 NVDA 營收與財測全面優於預期，且上修 FY2028 營收成長預期

資料來源：Bloomberg、FactSet 
 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
NVDA FY3Q27 營收、獲利及 Q4 財測都優於市場預期。財報公布後，股價
盤後一度上漲約 4%；董事會主席在法說會上表明不讓黃仁勳回任，漲幅一
度收斂。隨後暫代執行長 Kress 將 FY2028 營收成長指引由~70% 上修至
~75%，盤後漲幅再度擴大近 5%。一掃市場原先對公司可能會撤回長期展
望的擔憂；加上 Vera Rubin 創下公司史上最快放量，世代轉換十分順利。 
展望後市，市場普遍認為，AI 算力需求仍受供給限制，看好公司 FY2028 營
運能見度。CPU、網通與每座資料中心的產品含量提升，有助抵銷 ASIC 市
占提升的壓力；以 CY2027 本益比衡量，NVDA 評價仍低於費半。惟後續變
數在於 CEO 接班、美國新出口管制，以及相關調查與訴訟風險。整體而
言，基本面動能延續，惟治理與政策風險是評價折價的主因。 
公司財報重點數據： 
NVDA FY3Q27 營收 1,158 億美元，QoQ+20%、YoY+103%，優於市場預
期的 1,090 億美元。Non-GAAP 毛利率 74.9%，優於市場預期，主因產品組
合較佳。Non-GAAP EPS 2.68 美元，YoY+111%，優於市場預期的 2.47 美
元；自由現金流 437.8 億美元，較上季的 213.4 億美元明顯回升。本季以庫
藏股與股利合計返還股東 281 億美元（庫藏股約 220 億美元、現金股利約 
60 億美元），創歷史新高。董事會於 9 月核准增加 1,500 億美元庫藏股額
度，季末剩餘額度約 2,273 億美元，計畫於 FY2028 年底前執行完畢。 
業務部門表現： 
資料中心：營收 1,076 億美元，QoQ+21%、YoY+110%，優於市場預期。
其中 Vera Rubin 首季營收近 200 億美元，網通營收逾 210 億美元。 
⚫ 超大型雲端（Hyperscale ） 營收 600 億 美 元 ， QoQ+23% 、
YoY+71%，受惠 Vera Rubin 放量及 Grace Blackwell 需求延續。雲端
產業積壓訂單已由三個月前的 2 兆美元增至 2.5 兆美元。 
⚫ ACI&E（AI 雲端、工業、企業）營收 470 億美元，QoQ+18%、YoY
近 3 倍 。 季 增 動 能 來 自  Vera Rubin 出貨給主要新興 雲 端 業者
（Neocloud）及前沿模型實驗室自建資料中心。主權 AI 營收 YoY 逾 
3 倍；金融業地端營收過去 12 個月接近 50 億美元，年增逾 1 倍。 
⚫ 本季對中國出貨的授權 Hopper 資料中心產品營收為 24 億美元。 
邊緣運算：營收 82 億美元，QoQ+14%、YoY+42%，優於市場預期，主要
由企業端 Blackwell 工作站及實體 AI 邊緣運算需求帶動。

公司展望：
(1).預估 FY4Q27 營收 1,300 億美元（±3%），優於市場預期；本季多一
週，且財測未納入任何中國資料中心運算營收。(2).預估 FY4Q27 毛利率
71%~72%，大致符合市場預期。公司表示 4Q 為毛利率低點，因需履行舊
價格訂單，新價格要到新年度才實施；FY2028 預計回升至 72%~73%，與
上季說法一致。(3). 預估 FY4Q27 GAAP / Non-GAAP 營業費用約 110 億 /
107 億 美 元 ， 反 映 多 出 一 週 及 交 接 相 關 成 本 ； FY2027 稅 率 維 持
16%~18%。(4). FY2028 全年營收成長由約 70%上修至約 75%（考量需求
成長、供應鏈支援增加，以及客戶資料中心整備與資金取得情況後的結
果）。(5). 預期 FY2028 CPU 營收、企業營收都將倍增以上。Vera CPU 新
增 CoreWeave、Meta、Nscale、OpenAI 等客戶。 (6). 產品藍圖不變：
Vera Rubin Ultra 預計明年推出，Feynman 預計 2028 年推出。DSX POD

模組化 AI 工廠預計明年初開始出貨美國及中歐客戶。(7). 供應鏈：本季新增
與 載 板 及 先 進 封 裝 業 者 的 產 能 合 作 ， 採 購 承 諾 增 至  3,900 億 美 元 。
Neocloud 相關承諾 320 億美元，公司表示將嚴格評估後續投資、承諾與保
證。(8). 中國：已停止出貨授權 Hopper 資料中心產品。美國商務部本週公
布新出口管制規則，公司仍在評估，但表示需求遠大於供給，預期營運仍維
持強勁。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
(1). FY2028 營收增速上修依據：公司表示，上修反映需求持續成長及新增
的供給；新出口管制仍在評估，但對 75% 有信心。(2). CEO 交接：董事會
主席開場即表明不會讓黃仁勳回任，並已委託獵才公司啟動遴選，內外部人
選都會考慮，且不會倉促決定。公司強調「營運一切如常」，客戶與供應商
全力支持，記憶體與先進封裝產能皆已確保。(3). Neocloud 融資與保證條
款： 公司表示，保證需待客戶完成融資、資料中心可啟用後才生效，這只

是說明現行做法，並非條款變更；未來支持客戶將更具選擇性，資本配置以
庫藏股為優先。(4). 2028 年放緩疑慮與產能分配：針對美國政局變化可能
帶來的政策風險，公司表示 Agentic AI 需求將延續到 2028 年，並持續與政
府溝通。產能分配依客戶資料中心整備度、資金到位及承接能力而定，所有
客戶價格一致。
公司簡介、競爭對手與公司自身價值：
NVIDIA 已轉型為 AI 基礎設施平台業者，護城河建立在 GPU、Vera CPU、
NVLink／Spectrum-X 網通、CPO 共同封裝光學與 CUDA 軟體生態系的
「極端共同設計」。公司表示，Vera Rubin 的每瓦 token 產出為 GB300 的
10 倍 ， 客 戶 導 入 時 間 只 有 Grace Blackwell 的 一 半 。 公 司 並 宣 布 收 購
Hugging Face，把影響力延伸至開源模型分發層。面對雲端業者加速自研
ASIC，NVDA 憑藉全端平台、供應鏈產能布局及生態系融資機制，維持 AI

算力市場的主導地位。

For Pegatron, we expect 4Q26 revenue to reach NT$514bn (+32% QoQ, +61% YoY), as 
we expect to see more contribution from AI server going into 4Q26. We expect GM to 
slightly decrease to 4.2% (-0.3ppt QoQ and +0.1%ppt YoY). We continue to see 4Q26 
revenue with QoQ growth on a continuing ramp-up in AI server shipments as well as the 
continued peak season for its communication business. 
For Compal, we expect 4Q26 revenue to reach NT$282bn (+16% QoQ, +48% YoY) with 
increasing contribution from AI server  as the company continue to expand from 
motherboard (L6) to system level (L10/L11). We expect GM to slightly increase to 4.6% 
(+0.2ppt QoQ and -1.2ppt YoY) as the YoY contraction is due to shift in product mix (we 
expect PC business to account for 60% in 4Q26 vs. 71% in 4Q25).  
For Asus, we expect 4Q26 revenue to reach NT$283bn (+2% QoQ, +40% YoY), with solid 
growth from its server business, offsetting the weakness from its PC business, although 
we continue to expect a more premium PC product mix. We expect GM to decrease to 
14.8% (-1.6ppt QoQ and -1.1ppt YoY) due to the change in product mix.

Earnings revisions 
Quanta 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 3% to factor in 3Q26 results (we revise 
up our 2026E revenue estimates by 2%). We revise down 2027/28E earnings estimates 
by 7%/1% to reflect a product mix with higher contribution from the server business (we 
revise up our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 1/2%). We now expect the GM to contract 
going forward due to the higher server business contribution, thus revise down GPM by 
0.3/0.2/0.1ppt in 2026/27/28E.

Pegatron 
We revise down our 2026 earnings estimates by 20% to factor in 3Q26 results (we 
maintain our 2026E revenue estimates). We revise down 2027 earnings estimates by 5% 
to reflect a higher % for minority interest while revise up 2028 earnings estimates by 3% 
with improved GM (we revise down our 2027/28E revenue estimates by 9/6% to reflect 
a more conservative assumption for its communication business). We now expect the 
GM to remain stable going forward, thus revise up GPM by 0.4/0.7/0.5ppt in 
2026/27/28E.

now expect the GM to decline going forward with higher server contribution, thus revise 
down 0.5/0.8ppt in 2027/28E.

Investment Thesis & PT methodology and risks

Investment Thesis - Quanta 
Quanta is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, tablets, 
consumer electronics, servers, etc. We are Neutral rated on Quanta. We expect the 
company’s earnings to grow in 2026-28E, driven by 1) growing AI server demand from 
Quanta’s CSP customers, and 2) general server demand recovery. However, we see P/E 
valuation as fair at the current level, given the positives appear to have been mostly 
factored in the share price and the current upside to our target price is relatively lower 
compared to its peers under coverage. We would consider becoming more positive if the 
AI server demand from its CSP clients turn out to be above our expectations.

### Page 7

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Quanta 
We are Neutral rated on Quanta. Our 12-month target price of NT$280 is based on a 
10.7x 2027E P/E, which is the company’s 2018-23 average P/E.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market 
recovery; 2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger-/weaker-than-expected demand for general servers. 
Pegatron is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of 
smartphones, PCs, consumer electronics, and servers, etc. While we like Pegatron’s 
product line diversification into servers, AI servers, and automotive electronics, we are 
Sell rated as we see revenue contribution from AI server remaining small in near-term, 
and our 2027E target P/E is near the company’s current trading P/E. We expect 
smartphone business will remain the major revenue contributor from 2026E to 2028E, 
and we see the AI server related revenue contribution remains 4% in 2026E (vs. peers 
14%~87%). Catalysts for further upside will be its faster-than-expected progress in AI 
servers. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - Pegatron 
We are Sell rated on Pegatron with a 12-month target price of NT$71. Our TP is based 
on a 9.7x 2027E P/E, which is the company’s 2018-23 average P/E. 
Key upside risks: 1) stronger-than-expected smartphone market recovery, 2) 
faster-than-expected AI server ramp-up, and 3) stronger-than-expected demand on 
consumer electronics.

Investment thesis- Compal 
Compal is an ODM company focusing on the design and manufacturing of PCs, tablets, 
consumer electronics, servers, etc. While we like Compal’s solid position in the PC ODM 
market and its expansion into AI servers, we are Neutral rated as we see the contribution 
from AI servers as limited in the near-term given its limited exposure to cloud server 
customers, who are the early adopters of AI servers. Meanwhile, we believe valuation 
already reflects near-term positives. We model Compal earnings growth in 2026-26E to 
be mainly driven by commercial PC demand recovery and server business ramping up. 
We see P/E valuation as fair at current levels compared to peers given its mild earnings 
growth in 2026-28E.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Compal 
We are Neutral-rated on Compal with a 12-month target price of NT$45. Our target 
price is based on 14.7x 2027E P/E, which is +0.5 STDV above the company’s 2018-23 
average P/E. 
Key upside/downside risks: 1) stronger-/weaker-than-expected PC market recovery; 
2) faster-/slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up; and 3) 
stronger/weaker-than-expected demand for tablets.

### Page 8

Investment thesis- Asus 
Asus is a multinational computer hardware and electronics company based in Taiwan 
and is the top motherboard and gaming brand globally and also a top-three consumer 
notebook vendor, per the company. We are Neutral rated on Asus considering (1) 
challenging PC market under end of Win10 replacement cycle, rising memory costs, and 
consumption pull in in 1H25 under China government subsidies and tariff concerns, (2) 
ASUS brand AI servers remain small in shipment. The stock currently trades at 14x 2027E 
PE, close to our target PE multiple (14.8x), which we think is fair.  
We would turn more positive on ASUS if we see (1) its PC revenue growth stronger than 
expected which could be driven by faster than expected product mix upgrade toward AI 
PC or gaming PC, or (2) faster than expected AI servers shipment ramp up, which could 
be driven by penetration in leading enterprises or CSPs (cloud services providers).

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,000. Our target 
price is based on 14.8x 2027E P/E, which is +0.5 STDV above the company’s 2018-23 
average P/E 
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs

investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Document 126: 261120_daiwa_acter

Source PDF: all report pdf/261120_daiwa_acter.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-20
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

Summary: Acter held an investor conference on 20 November, after its 3Q26 results 
release on 9 November 2026.

We have a Buy (1) rating on the stock with a 12-month TP of TWD1,415, based on a target 
PER of 20x, applied to our 1-year-forward EPS. For more information on this company, 
please refer to our initiation report, Initiation: acting on the demand for cleanrooms, on 7 
October 2026.

What’s the impact 
• 3Q26 results: margin recovered faster than we expected. Acter’s gross margin came 
in at 24.3% (improved from 20.6% in 2Q26 and 17.8% in 3Q25), above our estimate 
(23.0%) and the consensus (24.1%), thanks to the revenue recognition of large-scale 
cases with costs partially recognised in 2Q26. Thus, the operating margin of 18.9% also 
beat our estimate (17.9%) but was slightly lower than the consensus (19.1%).

• Intact global demand for cleanroom technology, the edge AI market, and rapid 
cleanroom expansion in Asia further support Acter’s order backlog. Its backlog 
reached TWD84bn at the end of 3Q26 vs. TWD46bn in 2025 and TWD30bn in 2022 
(15.3% 2022-25 CAGR), which largely outpaced the global cleanroom technology 
market’s forecast CAGR. The company sees strong clean room demand in light of 
demand from the OSAT, ODM and memory segments but the labour shortage should be 
the bottle neck for the sector. In the near and medium term (up to 2 years), the company 
expects the gross margin to be 22-28%, subject to inflation. However, Acter foresees a 
favourable gross margin trend in the long run (ie. 3-5 years) on rising oversees revenue 
contribution. There should be some upside potential for gross margin from rush orders.

What we recommend 
We reaffirm our Buy (1) rating with a 12-month target price of TWD1,415, based on a 
target PER of 20x, applied to our 1-year forward EPS. Based on our 2026/27 EPS 
estimates, the stock is currently trading at PERs of 11.6x/8.4x, vs. its past-3-year trading 
range of 7-23x (average: 12x). Key downside risks: weaker-than-expected expansion in 
the semiconductor industry; project delays or cancellations.

In the interests of timeliness, this document has not been edited.

### Page 1

M
Idea
Delta Electronics Inc.  | Asia Pacific
Momentum to Pick Up in
Coming Quarters
We expect revenue to grow in  double digits (%) QoQ in both
4Q26 and 1Q27.  Low market expectations for HVDC in the 2H27
GPU platform skew risk/reward more  to the upside.
Revenue momentum picking up with better component supply: With October
monthly sales of NT$$74,666mn (+14% MoM/+30% YoY), we think Delta is on
tracking for 10-15% top-line growth QoQ for 4Q26, as component supply increases

and some power rack shipments  emerge, albeit with a relatively small contribution.
We expect the momentum to be sustained into 1Q27, likely another double-digit (%)
QoQ rise on the top line, along with the new GPU server ramp and postponed
shipments from past quarters. We anticipate  strong revenue growth in 2027, as
power consumption will increase significantly in the new GPU server racks. The
contributions from liquid cooling solutions and DC/DC modules will also be good

drivers.
HVDC power rack timing key to watch, but expectations not high: We expect
Delta to start shipping HVDC power racks to its customers from 4Q26, though the
contribution might be a low-single-digit (%) share of total revenue through 1H27, by
our estimate. All eyes are on the adoption of HVDC power racks in the next-
generation AI server platforms – we think power consumption at the rack level is at
the threshold of HVDC power racks being a necessity. Our checks indicate that most

supply chain players now see HVDC as the reference design for the GPU server
generation scheduled for 2H27. Though this does not appear finalized yet, the
chances of that happening should increase  moving closer to the launch. Moreover,
we believe that the base case for most people is for HVDC not to be the only power
delivery structure in that 2H27 GPU server platform, which points to potential
upside in 2H27/2028.
Liquid cooling and fuel cell good complementary businesses: Delta has been a

dominant supplier in liquid cooling solutions, which should show adoption
proliferating from computing/switch trays to more devices, including power shelves,
optical transceivers, etc. For the fuel cell business, we believe Delta's expertise in
power delivery could help it offer more integrated solutions. Smaller-scale projects
should start from 2027 and could grow into a much more meaningful revenue
stream. The board  approved a greenfield project in Guanyin (Taiwan) with  capex of

~NT$10bn back in May 2026. We expect fuel cells to reach a multi-billion dollar (US
$) revenue scale in the coming few years. .
Stay OW: Current valuation is 16x 2027e P/E vs. the trough level of 14x. We view
risk-reward as attractive, given Delta's dominant position in AI power/cooling related
offerings. We stay OW on the stock and believe that accelerating momentum in the
near term should help it outperform. Our NT$2,300 price target is based on 30x

Morgan Stanley appreciates your support in
the 2027 Extel Asia Research Survey.
Request your ballot                          here.

Valuation Methodology
Our price target (base case scenario value) is NT$2,300, derived from 30x average
2027-2028e EPS: We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it appropriately captures Delta's earnings growth potential. We believe our target
P/E multiple of 30x is justified by an earnings CAGR of 59% over 2025-28e, which implies
a PEG of 0.5. This is lower than the average of 1.0 in 2022-25, when the stock was trading

at an average P/E of 23x for a 23% earnings CAGR.
Bull case scenario value – NT$3,050: We apply a 40x P/E multiple   to our average base
case estimate for 2027-28. This scenario assumes a greater-than-expected contribution
from higher-margin businesses such as power electronics and liquid cooling as well as
greater synergies from acquired companies.
Bear case scenario value – NT$920 (from NT$1,050):  We apply a 12x P/E multiple  (from
14x) to our average base case estimate for 2027-28. This scenario assumes further profit

drag from legacy products, smaller profit contribution from higher-margin businesses,
higher opex and/or less meaningful contribution from acquired companies.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

hyungkeun.ryu@daishin.com

투자의견 BUY  
매수, 유지 
6개월 목표주가 500,000 
유지  
현재주가 
(26.10.29) 
231,000 
반도체업종

◼ 3분기 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 
◼ 주주환원은 시점의 문제일 뿐. 강화 방향성은 여전히 유효. 
◼ 메모리반도체 이익의 폭증이 가격의 매력을 유발. 비중확대 전략 유효.

매력적인 가격, 변하지 않은 주주환원 정책의 방향성 
목표주가와 매수의견 유지. 메모리반도체 주도 폭발적 이익 상승이 가격 (주
가)의 매력을 지속 유발하고 있고, 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향성에도 변한 것
은 없다는 판단. 절대적으로 싼 주가를 비중 확대의 기회로 삼을 필요. 
1. 주주환원, 시점의 문제 
금번 실적발표에 대한 시장의 반응은 다소 냉소적. 대외 불확실성이 큰 만큼, 
시장은 자사주 매입/소각과 같은 즉각적 주가 부양 정책의 발표를 기대.  
당사는 삼성전자의 주주환원이 “시점의 문제”라 생각. 총 주주환원의 규모도, 
차기 (2027-2029년) 주주환원 정책의 강화 방향에도 변한 것은 없다는 판
단. 시장이 기대하는 자사주 매입/소각은 우선주 중심으로 시행될 가능성이 
높으며, 시행 시, 그 규모는 25조원 내외일 것 (DPS 12,567원)으로 전망. 
2. 메모리반도체, 더 올라갈 것 
보수적 환율 전망 (2027년 평균 환율 1,300원 적용)에도, 2027년 메모리반도
체 영업이익은 608조원에 달할 것. 이익 전망의 상향 여력 또한 유효. 
1) 최대 생산, 최대 판매 
삼성전자는 금일 4Q26 DRAM 판매량 가이던스로 전분기 대비 10% 중후반 
성장 (q-q +18%로 추정)을 제시. 일부 투자자들은 높아진 기저 부담으로 
2027년 판매 성장이 제한될 것을 우려. 당사는 높아진 기저에도 2027년 
DRAM 판매가 전년대비 20% 상승할 것으로 전망. 
- 극단적 수급 불균형 속, 삼성전자는 “최대 생산, 최대 판매” 정책을 시행 
중. 삼성전자만의 강력한 Execution이 20% 대 성장을 유지시킬 원동력. 
- DRAM 1c 수율의 조기 개선 속, 범용 DRAM 양산에 DRAM 1c Capa가 일
부 투입될 것으로 예상. Bit 성장을 높일 수 있는 변화가 될 것. 
2) 열려 있는 가격의 상방 
1Q27 모바일 DRAM에서 Surprise가 발생할 것이라는 의견을 유지 (전분기 
대비 30% 이상 상승). 장기계약이 체결된 CSP향으로도 가격의 추가 상승 여
력이 존재한다는 점을 상기할 필요. CSP의 경우, 당장의 비용 부담보다 AI 시
장 선점을 우선시. 장기계약으로 형성된 가격 Band의 천장 (Ceiling) 또한 시
장 우려 대비 낮지 않음을 감안할 필요. 2027년 범용 DRAM ASP는 전년대비 
40% 중후반까지 상승할 가능성 (현 전망: 전년대비 34% 상승).  
3) HBM, 제품 경쟁력과 점유율의 동반 성장  
최근 삼성전자향으로 HBM4/HBM4e 추가 공급 요청이 쇄도하고 있는 것으
로 추정. 일부 고객은 연내 추가 공급 시 2027년 계약 가격을 선제 지급할 
의사를 표출. 제품 경쟁력이 회복되었음을 보여주는 증거라 생각. 
제품 경쟁력의 회복과 최대 Capa의 이점 속, 2027년은 HBM 점유율 1위를 
확보하는 기념비적 해가 될 것으로 전망 (매출액 기준 점유율 38% 예상).

KOSPI 5937.66

두산에너빌리티 (034020)

EARNINGS REVIEW Company Analysis | 건설/원전 | 2026. 10. 30  
숫자로 증명하는 중

3Q26 Review: 에너빌부문 믹스개선, 두산밥캣 호실적 
두산에너빌리티 3분기 매출액은 4조 4,494억원으로 전년동기대비 14.7% 증가, 컨센
서스에 부합했다. 영업이익은 2,612억원으로 전년동기대비 90.6% 증가, 컨센서스 영
업이익 2,464억원 대비 6.0% 상회하는 호실적을 기록했다. 에너빌리티부문의 원자
력, 가스터빈 매출 증가에 따른 믹스 개선 효과와 두산밥캣의 가격정책 효과에 힘입은 
마진 개선으로 안정적인 실적 기조가 이어지고 있어 긍정적이다. 분기 환평가손실로 
인해 순이익은 여타 수출기업과 유사하게 컨센서스 대비 하회했다. 에너빌부문의 수주
는 누계 10조원으로 목표대비 75%를 채웠으며, 수주 대기중인 안건들을 고려할 때 
당분간 수주 업사이클이 몇년간 지속될 것임이 분명한 시점이다. 
기회가 아닌 경쟁력, 모멘텀이 아닌 수주  
두산에너빌리티에 대해 투자의견 Buy 유지하나, 목표주가를 95,000원으로 하향했다. 
목표 EBITDA는 2035년 EBITDA에 WACC 8%를 적용, 목표 EV/EBITDA는 Global 
Peers인 GE Vernova의 2026년 EV/EBITDA 멀티플에서 자회사 실적 변동성을 고
려해 10% 할인 적용했다. 호실적과 수주 증가에도 GEV의 멀티플 감소와 순차입금 
증가로 인한 NAV 감소를 밸류에이션에 반영한 결과이다. 전일 주가는 한수원의 체코 
원전 시공사 협상 결렬로 인해 7.65% 하락 마감했다. 시공 수주는 아쉽지만 두산에너
빌리티의 체코 원전 기자재 납품에는 전혀 영향이 없으며, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가로 경
제적해자인 대형 가스 터빈 수급 쇼티지는 당분간 지속될 전망임을 고려할 때 전일 주
가는 낙폭 과대로 판단한다. 1) 두산에너빌리티의 가스터빈은 이미 2031년 출하 물량
에 대한 예약 주문이 마무리되고 있으며, 2) 또한 AP1000의 우량한 수주 파이프라인
과, 3) 뉴스케일파워, 테라파워, 롤스로이스, X Energy 등의 SMR 제작을 위한 협업 역
시 지속되고 있다는 점에 주목한다. 전일 기사를 통해 뉴스케일파워 SMR 1호 제작 계
약을 체결했다는 소식을 전했으며, 이제 에너지 트렌지션은 기회가 아닌 회사의 경쟁
력으로, 가스터빈과 원전은 모멘텀이 아니라 수주로 입증되고 있다.  
 Analyst 김세련 
sally.kim@ls-sec.co.kr

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
SK 하이닉스 최대주주 
SK 하이닉스 4 분기도 배당 주당 20,000 원 이상  
SK 하이닉스는 전일 주당 20,000 원(실적반영: 10,000 원, 키옥시아 특별배당: 
10,000원)의 3분기 배당을 발표하였다. 키옥시아 특별배당은 3분기 배당과 26년 회
계연도 결산배당으로 나누어 지급예정이다. 이에 SK 스퀘어는 2조 9,220억원(1분기
와 2분기 배당수입은 각 548억원)의 배당수입을 수취하게 된다. SK하이닉스는 26년 
전체 현금 배당 규모는 30조원 이상을 발표했는데, 3분기까지 지급하는 배당금은 14.6
조원이기 때문에 4분기에도 최소한 주당 20.000원 이상의 배당이 예상된다. 이 경우 
SK 스퀘어의 26 년 배당수입은 6 조원에 달할 것으로 예상한다. SK 스퀘어는 ‘26 년
~’28년 경상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 주주환원정책을 보유하고 있
어 SK스퀘어의 현 시가총액 대비 27년 주주환원율은 최소 1.5% 이상 될 전망이다.

SK 하이닉스 자사주 취득·소각으로 높아질 지배력 
SK하이닉스는 20조원의 자사주 매입을 발표하였다. 이 중 10조원은 임직원 보상목적
이며, 10조원은 주주환원 목적이다. 26년 총 60조원 이상의 자사주 취득·소각 예정(8
월 결의분 40 조원 포함)으로 임직원 보상 목적의 자사주 매입은 제외되기 때문에 26
년 실적 확정 후 추가적으로 10조원 이상의 자사주 취득이 예상된다. 기 취득한 자사주 
40조원(3.3%)는 11월 5일 소각 예정으로 소각 이후 SK스퀘어의 SK하이닉스 지분
율은 20.5%(2 분기말 기준)에서 21.2%로 상승하게 된다. 이번 발표한 10 조원
(1.0%) 자사주 소각시 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 21.4%이 되며, 추가로 10 조원을 추가 
소각한다면 21.7%까지 지분율이 상승하게 된다.

SK 하이닉스 대비 높은 베타 
SK스퀘어에 대한 투자의견 매수를 유지한다. SK스퀘어 NAV의 98%가량을 차지하
는 SK하이닉스의 현금배당 증가로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 재원이 확대되고, SK하이닉
스의 자사주 취득·소각으로 지배력이 강화되기 때문이다. SK하이닉스는 전일 컨퍼런스 
콜을 통해 29년까지 D램과 HBM 물량이 사실상 모두 솔드아웃된 상황이라 밝히며 주
가 반등 가능성이 높은데, SK 스퀘어 베타(1.8)는 SK 하이닉스(1.7) 대비 높기 때문에 
SK 하이닉스 주가 반등에 따른 SK 스퀘어의 동반 주가 상승을 기대할 수 있다. 현재 
SK스퀘어의 NAV 대비 할인율은 46.5%이며, SK하이닉스가 자사주 60조원 소각시 
할인율은 49.4%까지 상승하게 된다.

2026-10-29

지주회사/통신서비스/ESG. 최관순 / ks1.choi@sks.co.kr / 3773-8812

작성자(최관순)는 본 조사분석자료에 게재된 내용들이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 신의성실하게 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 보고서에 언급된 종목의 경우 당사 조사분석담당자는 본인의 담당종목을 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
본 보고서는 기관투자가 또는 제 3 자에게 사전 제공된 사실이 없습니다. 
당사는 자료공표일 현재 해당기업과 관련하여 특별한 이해 관계가 없습니다. 
종목별 투자의견은 다음과 같습니다. 
투자판단 3 단계(6 개월기준) 15%이상 → 매수 / -15%~15% → 중립 / -15%미만 → 매도

Document 316: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53.2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities in the coming 6-12 months, 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15-20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid-to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid-cycle bull market. Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex-Japan (AxJ) equities, which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

Allocation to global equities decrease to 52.5% from 
53.2% for our CIO Foundation Balanced portfolio

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex-UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low-teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities. We expect the 
segment to generate 10-15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years, with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

US and Asia ex-Japan would continue to lead in 
earnings growth in 2027

### Page 3

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High-Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks, MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advances in oncology and innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks 
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. Healthcare and 
consumer staples accounts for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management in a mid-
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and potentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge 
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

on average

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two, and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets, private equity provides a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, infrastructure offers differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where contracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3-7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell-off caused by AI-
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi-sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010-2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress.

### Page 1

TCT Cảng Hàng không Việt Nam (ACV) 
Áp lực lợi nhuận trong chu kỳ mở rộng công suất 
19/11/2026

KQKD 3Q2026 suy giảm do khấu hao 
và biến động tỷ giá

Lượt khách ACV dự kiến tăng chậm 
hơn toàn ngành do tác động từ Phú 
Quốc và Gia Bình

Long Thành dự kiến khai thác từ cuối 
2026, tạo áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
giai đoạn đầu

Duy trì khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, giá 
mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu

3Q2026, ACV ghi nhận doanh thu đạt 6,066 tỷ VND (-6% yoy) và LNST công ty mẹ 
đạt 2,206 tỷ VND (-14% yoy). Lợi nhuận suy giảm chủ yếu do (1) khấu hao tăng từ 
các nhà ga mới và (2) ghi nhận lỗ tỷ giá 168 tỷ VND trong kỳ, trái ngược với khoản lãi 
tỷ giá cùng kỳ 2025. Lũy kế 9M2026, doanh thu và LNST công ty mẹ lần lượt đạt 
19,226/7,435 tỷ VND (+0%/+2% yoy).

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa qua hệ thống ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân 7%/5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc 
không còn thuộc hệ thống ACV từ đầu 2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội 
Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang sân bay Gia Bình khi đi vào khai thác. Mức độ dịch 
chuyển trước năm 2030 dự kiến còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện 
sân bay và hạ tầng giao thông kết nối.

Cảng HKQT Long Thành giai đoạn 1 dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 
12/2026 và bắt đầu tiếp nhận các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
vào ngày 28/03/2027, bổ sung công suất 25 triệu khách/năm cho ACV. Tuy nhiên, 
tốc độ chuyển dịch có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do hạ tầng kết nối chưa hoàn thiện, 
trong khi chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng dự kiến gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận 
của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028.

Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền, chúng tôi duy trì khuyến nghị TRUNG 
LẬP đối với ACV với giá mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng 
giá 4.5% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 18/11/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

ACV hiện đang quản lý, đầu tư và khai thác 
hệ thống 21 Cảng hàng không trong cả nước, 
bao gồm 10 Cảng hàng không quốc tế và 11 
Cảng hàng không nội địa. Ngành nghề kinh 
doanh chính của ACV là hoạt động cung cấp 
dịch vụ hỗ trợ trực tiếp cho vận tải hàng 
không, bao gồm dịch vụ hàng không, dịch vụ 
phi hàng không và bán hàng. 
Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Điểm nhấn đầu tư  Chú thích 
Tăng trưởng bền vững đến từ cả nhóm khách nội địa và quốc tế, tốc độ tăng trung bình dự kiến đạt 
5.5% và 7%/năm giai đoạn 2026-2030. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Long Thành bổ sung 25 triệu khách/năm từ 2027, song khấu hao tăng mạnh khiến lợi nhuận chạm 
đáy năm 2028 trước khi phục hồi từ 2029. 
 Chi tiết ở phần dưới của báo cáo 
Quá trình lên HOSE của ACV tiến triển thuận lợi nhờ các tài sản nhà nước đang dần được tách bạch, 
tạo thuận lợi cho việc hoàn tất cổ phần hóa tài sản khu bay cho phần vốn nhà nước. 
 Tham khảo trong báo cáo kỳ trước tại đây

Biểu đồ 6. Các quốc gia có lượng khách đến Việt Nam lớn nhất trong 10M2026 (khách) 
Trung Quốc và Hàn Quốc tiếp tục là hai thị 
trường khách quốc tế lớn nhất đến Việt Nam 
trong 10M2026 nhưng tăng trưởng hạn chế 
(+1.4% và -6.3% yoy), chủ yếu chịu tác động 
từ giá nhiên liệu tăng cao, khiến các hãng 
hàng không cắt giảm tần suất bay và du 
khách có xu hướng ưu tiên các điểm đến gần. 
Ngược lại, khách châu Âu tăng mạnh (+51.5% 
yoy), chủ yếu nhờ (1) thị trường Nga phục hồi 
(+150% yoy) sau khi các đường bay thẳng và 
charter được khôi phục, mở rộng và (2) hưởng 
lợi từ chính sách miễn thị thực bổ sung cho 12 
quốc gia châu Âu từ tháng 8/2025 và việc tăng 
cường kết nối hàng không trong năm 2026.

Nguồn: GSO

Triển vọng tăng trưởng hành khách 
tích cực, song ACV dự kiến tăng 
chậm hơn toàn ngành 
 Theo dự báo của Bộ Xây dựng, lượng hành khách thông qua các cảng hàng 
không Việt Nam dự kiến tăng trưởng bình quân 9.7%/năm giai đoạn 2026–
2030, đạt 191.2 triệu lượt vào năm 2030. Về dài hạn, Airbus dự báo lưu lượng 
hành khách hàng không Việt Nam tăng trưởng 7.7%/năm trong 20 năm tới, gần 
gấp khoảng 2 lần mức trung bình toàn cầu (3.9%/năm). Các động lực chính bao 
gồm triển vọng tăng trưởng kinh tế, nhu cầu du lịch gia tăng, chính sách miễn 
thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng đường bay quốc tế, cùng kế hoạch bổ 
sung đội bay của các hãng hàng không.

### Page 5

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế và nội địa của ACV tăng trưởng bình 
quân lần lượt 7% và 5.5%/năm đến 2030, thấp hơn mức tăng trưởng chung của 
toàn ngành do (1) sân bay Phú Quốc được chuyển ra khỏi hệ thống ACV từ đầu 
2026 và (2) một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia 
Bình khi sân bay này đi vào khai thác. Chúng tôi đánh giá mức độ dịch chuyển 
trước năm 2030 còn hạn chế do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ xây dựng sân bay và hạ 
tầng kết nối. Bên cạnh đó, đề xuất chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài 
sang Gia Bình hiện chưa được phê duyệt và chưa được phản ánh trong dự 
phóng. Nếu được thông qua, đề xuất này có thể ảnh hưởng đáng kể đến lợi 
nhuận của ACV, do lợi nhuận trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao gấp khoảng 4–5 lần 
khách nội địa, đặc biệt trong giai đoạn sau năm 2030.

Nguồn: ACV, KBSV tổng hợp  Nguồn: ACV, KBSV tổng hợp

Cảng HKQT Long Thành mở ra dư 
địa tăng trưởng dài hạn, song tạo 
áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong 
ngắn và trung hạn 
 Cảng HKQT Long Thành dự kiến khai thác một phần từ cuối tháng 12/2026, chủ 
yếu phục vụ các chuyến bay nội địa và một số chuyến quốc tế. Hoạt động 
chuyển các chuyến bay quốc tế đường dài và vận tải hàng hóa từ Tân Sơn Nhất 
dự kiến bắt đầu từ ngày 28/03/2027. Tuy nhiên, chúng tôi đánh giá tốc độ dịch 
chuyển có thể chậm hơn kế hoạch do phụ thuộc vào tiến độ hoàn thiện hạ tầng 
giao thông kết nối với trung tâm TP.HCM, đặc biệt là dự án mở rộng cao tốc 
TP.HCM – Long Thành – Dầu Giây và các dự án kết nối đường sắt bao gồm Thủ 
Thiêm – Long Thành.

Dự án cảng HKQT Long Thành kỳ vọng sẽ mở ra dư địa tăng trưởng dài hạn cho 
ACV, dù gây áp lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu khai thác. Việc đưa 
Long Thành vào vận hành sẽ giúp ACV mở rộng công suất, giảm áp lực quá tải 
tại Tân Sơn Nhất, đồng thời tạo điều kiện thu hút thêm các đường bay quốc tế, 
đặc biệt là đường bay dài và hoạt động vận tải hàng hóa. Tuy nhiên, trong ngắn 
và trung hạn, chi phí khấu hao và vận hành tăng trong khi sản lượng hành 
khách cần thời gian để đạt công suất tối ưu dự kiến sẽ gây áp lực lên biên lợi 
nhuận của ACV giai đoạn 2027–2028. Trong mô hình dự phóng, chúng tôi ghi 
nhận khấu hao Long Thành theo tiến độ nghiệm thu, kết chuyển và đưa vào sử 
dụng từng hạng mục tài sản cố định, thay vì toàn bộ dự án ngay khi bắt đầu vận 
hành.

chuyến/tuần, gồm 322 nội địa và 44 quốc tế.

11/2026.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị TRUNG LẬP, 
giá mục tiêu 39,500 VND/cổ phiếu  
 Sử dụng phương pháp chiết khấu dòng tiền, chúng tôi duy trì khuyến nghị 
TRUNG LẬP đối với cổ phiếu ACV trong bối cảnh rủi ro từ đề xuất chuyển các 
chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài sang Gia Bình gia tăng. Giá mục tiêu của ACV là 
39,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương với mức tăng giá tiềm năng 4.5% so với mức 
giá đóng cửa 37,800 VND/cổ phiếu ngày 18/11/2026. Các giả định chính bao 
gồm: 
- Giai đoạn 2026–2030, dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế/nội địa qua hệ 
thống cảng hàng không ACV tăng trưởng bình quân 7%/5.5%/năm, 
thấp hơn tốc độ tăng trưởng chung của ngành hàng không Việt Nam, 
chủ yếu do (1) ACV không còn ghi nhận sản lượng hành khách tại sân 
bay Phú Quốc và (2) giả định một phần khách quốc tế tại Nội Bài dịch 
chuyển tự nhiên sang Gia Bình với mức độ còn hạn chế do hạ tầng kết 
nối chưa hoàn thiện. Dự phóng chưa phản ánh đề xuất chuyển các 
chuyến bay quốc tế sang Gia Bình. 
- Giá dịch vụ trung bình cảng hàng không dự kiến sẽ điều chỉnh tăng 2% 
mỗi 4 năm. 
- Dự án Long Thành giai đoạn 1 và giai đoạn 2 bắt đầu đưa vào khai thác 
từ cuối 2026 và đầu 2032, gia tăng doanh thu nhưng đồng thời tạo áp 
lực lên biên lợi nhuận trong giai đoạn đầu vận hành do chi phí khấu 
hao và vận hành tăng.

### Page 8

Rủi ro giảm đối với giá mục tiêu ACV chủ yếu đến từ khả năng đề xuất chuyển 
các chuyến bay quốc tế từ Nội Bài sang Gia Bình được phê duyệt. Kịch bản cơ 
sở hiện chỉ phản ánh mức dịch chuyển tự nhiên. Trong trường hợp đề xuất 
được thông qua, sản lượng khách quốc tế qua hệ thống ACV có thể thấp hơn 
đáng kể so với dự phóng, kéo theo suy giảm doanh thu và lợi nhuận do khả 
năng sinh lời trên mỗi khách quốc tế cao hơn khoảng 4–5 lần so với khách nội 
địa.

Biểu đồ 13. P/B, ROE lịch sử

P/B của ACV tiếp tục giảm về quanh 1.8–1.9x, 
chạm và có lúc xuống dưới ngưỡng -2std. Đây 
là vùng thấp nhất 5 năm và chiết khấu sâu so 
với mức trung bình 5 năm, phản ánh lo ngại 
về áp lực khấu hao và chi phí khi Long Thành 
đi vào vận hành.

Authors 
Jim Reid 
Global Head of Macro and 
Thematic Research

David Folkerts-Landau, Ph.D. 
Group Chief Economist &  
Global Head of Research

November 24, 2026 
2027: Great Expectations, but Risky Business 
Great Expectations, but Risky Business feels an appropriate title for our 
2027 World Outlook, especially after last week’s savage equity sell-off. We 
remain firmly of the view that AI will have a significant productivity impact, 
and the evidence is becoming more supportive: AI-related capex is already 
large enough to influence US growth, while early signs point to stronger 
productivity in more AI-intensive sectors. History nevertheless warns that 
capex-led technological revolutions rarely travel smoothly to the promised 
land. Overinvestment, busts and unexpected winners and losers are part of 
the journey. 
Geopolitics could again be responsible for some of the largest forecast 
errors. Pressure for a Russia-Ukraine ceasefire may increase, but European 
security challenges will persist; US-China competition is likely to intensify 
across strategic technologies; and the Middle East remains an important 
source of energy risk. 
That backdrop matters for bonds. Our rates strategists are now broadly 
neutral on duration, while expecting steeper yield curves. Rising government 
supply, large fiscal demands and vulnerability to supply shocks will should 
keep equilibrium rates and term premia structurally above 2010s norms. 
Overall, the same forces producing the upside are also creating the risks. AI 
could deliver the productivity breakthrough the developed world has been 
waiting decades for, but the investment required to get there creates the 
possibility of overinvestment. Fiscal activism can support growth, but adds 
to bond supply and debt. Greater strategic autonomy can boost resilience, 
but requires huge spending on defence, energy and technology, while 
adding to geopolitical tensions. 
If our great expectations are fulfilled, 2027 could ultimately be a very good 
year. But don't expect it to feel like that every day in what is likely to be 
another volatile year. 
Full report available for clients of Deutsche Bank Research here: World 
Outlook 2027: Great Expectations, but Risky Business

Document 445: ‘Double-you’ could well be true (but are we past the ‘J’_) (2026-11-28)

Source PDF: all report pdf/‘Double-you’ could well be true (but are we past the ‘J’_) (2026-11-28).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-28
Pages: 12
Status: ok

### Page 1

#australia #luciellis #technology #productivity
‘Double-you’ could well be true (but are we past the ‘J’?)
By Luci Ellis
Chief Economist Westpac Group, Westpac
08:55 November 27 2026 Share
There is a learning curve for any new technology, but we could be closer to seeing the
economy-wide productivity benefits than many people think. This is less-good news for the
producers’ returns, though.
Tech stocks have sold off as the scale of the AI / data centre build-out has collided with doubts about

future revenues. Nobody adopts a new technology unless they are better off doing so. This limits how

### Page 2

Tech stocks sold off in recent weeks, as the AI build-out’s demand for resources and debt collided with
uncertainty about future revenues, and concerns about some founders’ judgement. Prices partially
rebounded when Jensen Huang was reinstated as NVIDIA CEO, but the uncertainty remains. That
uncertainty in turn stems from doubts over the ultimate productivity benefit, and about who will capture
those gains. If AI doesn’t produce the hoped-for wins, or if end-users reap most of the benefits, the

labs’ revenue projections might be overly optimistic. Anthropic’s document filing ahead of its IPO
concentrated attention on this question, though the scepticism and ‘bubble’ discourse has been around
for a couple of years already.
Nobody adopts a technology unless they are better off doing so. At least some of the gains must
therefore accrue to the end-user firms and consumers or they will not adopt it. Plenty of interesting
technologies, such as blockchain or the Metaverse, have not broken out of niches because they were

not solutions to problems many people had. Modern AI is clearly not in that category, though:
businesses and consumers alike have adopted it rapidly, especially this year. But they will only keep
doing so if the cost makes the switch worthwhile. The end-user must be better off, and this caps the
revenue capture available to the producer.
Another strand of scepticism about the technology downplays the productivity benefits, at least those
accruing in the next couple of years. This scepticism is understandable. It takes time to learn how to

use any new technology effectively. A ‘J-curve’ can arise, where productivity initially dips before rising
higher than the pre-adoption level. The economics literature has highlighted how J-curves can emerge,
including for AI.
The learning phase for AI might be expected to be particularly drawn out because of the so-called
‘jagged frontier’, where the models are amazing at some tasks such as coding but poor at others. The
rapid evolution of the strongest models over the past 12 months worsens this issue. Your intuition

about what the models can and should be used for must be continually updated.
much of the benefit accrues to the producers of the technology. Low switching costs and lack of
brand loyalty also mean producers will not have much pricing power.
Some negativity about the technology stems from scepticism about its productivity benefits. The
‘jagged frontier’ of a technology that is amazing for some tasks but terrible at others exacerbates the
scepticism. This, and the rapid pace of improvement of the technology lead some observers to focus

on the learning curve involved in its adoption. A ‘J-curve’ can arise where productivity dips before
increasing. The history of technology waves also leads them to expect a long lag before the
productivity benefits are seen in economy-wide data.
We believe that the productivity increase could be evident within a couple of years. The LLM
chatbot era is more like the introduction of Windows 95 and the web, which democratised computing,
than it is the introduction of PCs a decade or more earlier. The lag from introduction to benefits being

evident could be similarly short, plausibly by 2028. Even if the productivity benefit of AI is not literally
a ‘double-you’ situation, but more like that late 1990s productivity boom, there are reasons to expect
those benefits to arrive sooner than some think.

### Page 3

In addition, the world is in the ‘Gin Lane’ phase of getting used to a new product, with all the bad and
antisocial consequences – cyber risk, slop and fraud to name a few – arising ahead of the systems to
manage them. (And if the ‘Gin Lane’ metaphor holds, you might expect AI ‘Temperance’ movements to
emerge and move the pendulum too far the other way before a balanced is reached.)
So far past Solow
If we grant that there will be a ‘J-curve’ on productivity from AI, does that mean the benefits are still

some years away? People well recall the famous ‘Solow paradox’ that ‘computers are everywhere
except in the productivity statistics’. Likewise, there was a multidecade lag between the introduction of
electric power and the productivity boost in manufacturing. We do not think it is correct to extrapolate
from those episodes, though. There are reasons to expect at least some discernible effects on
economy-wide productivity measures within two years.

It comes down to timing. With AI adoption, many people start the clock in 2022 with ChatGPT’s release,
but that might be the wrong starting point.
Consider that when Solow made his famous quip in 1987, most personal computers still used clunky
MS-DOS. Windows 3.0 was three years away still. Windows 95, the product that mainstreamed easy-
to-use interfaces, was eight years away. Macs and laser printers existed and were revolutionising print
publishing, but given their price, did not break out of that niche for many years. While it pains this long-

time Mac user to admit, the introduction of Windows 95 and dial-up internet was the ‘good enough’
technology that accelerated growth in productivity. The lag to that being visible in the data was just a
few years. Productivity growth had already picked up by the late 1990s.
We think that the introduction of LLMs and chat interfaces maps more closely to the introduction of
Windows 95, HTML and the web, and retail internet connections in the mid-1990s than to the advent of

personal computing more than a decade earlier. On that basis, the productivity benefits are likely to
become evident relatively quickly – perhaps not this year, but plausibly by 2028.
Like Windows 95 to computing, LLMs and their chat and coding interfaces have democratised access
to machine learning models. Back in 2018, I expressed scepticism that machine learning and AI would
be widely adopted. Along with its sister discipline ‘data science’, AI at the time was an exercise in

replacing relatively abundant skills – the bachelor-degree or high-school level skills required of most
white-collar jobs – with the much scarcer PhD-level skills in mathematics, statistics and computer
science needed at the time to build and tune these models. Replacing abundant skills with scarcer,
more expensive ones never works outside rare niches.
The LLM-with-harness revolution has changed my mind. It has super-charged the already skilled –

who would have expected Navier–Stokes and the Riemann Hypothesis to both be cracked in the same
year? More importantly, though, it has democratised access to ‘good enough’ versions of specialist
skills. There is a price to be paid for this advance in the form of slop, but clearly a benefit as well.

### Page 4

Related articles
There will be some learning curve still, and a blurring of boundaries between jobs, just as the Mac and
desktop publishing got more people thinking about fonts and graphic design, rather than delegate
those choices to a specialist. The end result will be a significant lift in productivity, and not in a decade’s
time but sooner.
This will not, however, necessarily mean model developers make all the money. Unlike computer
operating systems as a platform for applications, AI models have no ‘moat’: there are no network

economics creating a ‘winner-take-more’ effect, and no lock-in from an installed base. The interface
means there is little learning curve to switch between models. You might need to check your output
against the output from the model you were previously using, but that is an issue with new models from
the same lab, too. Many users switch around models easily and readily.
Waves of enthusiasm for the latest model from one or another of the main labs, including in the past

week, or a new open-weights lab, demonstrates that the most engaged users of these tools have little
brand loyalty. The main barrier to entry to this market is the setup costs of training a new model, but
there are already enough players, both at the frontier and the fast-follower open-weights group, to keep
competition intense.
Easy switching and willingness to do so add up to little pricing power for producers. This is good news
for economy-wide productivity, not so much for long-term profitability and R&D spend for independent

model producers.
The productivity benefits of AI are coming, perhaps sooner than some think. The result might not
be literally ‘double-you’ exponential growth. A period of stronger global productivity growth on the scale
of the late 1990s would still be a much better outcome for the global and Australian economies than the
past couple of decades.

Document 446: “AI股神”高喊抄底：AI实验室2027年底年化收入或突破万亿美元，Anthropic被严重低估，机器人ChatGPT时刻明年到来！

Source PDF: all report pdf/“AI股神”高喊抄底：AI实验室2027年底年化收入或突破万亿美元，Anthropic被严重低估，机器人ChatGPT时刻明年到来！.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-28
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Aschenbrenner旗下基⾦经历过⼭⻋⾏情后，仍⾼调喊出"现在是买⼊最佳时机"。他重仓
Anthropic并称浮盈14.8倍、估值仍被严重低估，据其估算Anthropic在2027年底年化经常
性收⼊（ARR）上的市销率不⾜2倍，在2027年底年化算⼒利润上的市盈率不⾜4倍，同时
更⼤胆预⾔2027年AI实验室年化收⼊将突破万亿美元。
摘要：
“AI股神”、前OpenAI研究员Leopold Aschenbrenner旗下Situational Awareness基⾦在三季度遭
遇重挫后，仍在其致投资者信中对AI赛道保持⾼度乐观，并将Anthropic列为核⼼持仓，称其估
值"极度低估"。
据此前披露的投资者信函，该基⾦上半年录得439%的惊⼈回报，但三季度净值回撤65%，截⾄
9⽉底全年收益收窄⾄88.5%。尽管如此，Aschenbrenner在信中再度向投资者喊话，称"现在是
买⼊的最佳时机"，并对AI进展速度及实验室商业价值作出⼀系列⼤胆预测。
在持仓结构上，随着最⼤私募持仓Anthropic完成IPO，该组合⽬前约70%为公开股票、30%为

私募公司，与历史⽔平⼀致。Aschenbrenner表⽰，这⼀格局与8⽉以私募为主的结构"正好相
反"。值得注意的是，他对英伟达、AMD、微软、苹果等当前市场主流标的均持看空态度，理由
是正在开发中的超级智能AI系统将彻底改变⾏业格局中的赢家。
Anthropic：持仓浮盈14.8倍，估值仍被严重低估
Aschenbrenner在信中将Anthropic视为其最重要的投资标的，并披露该持仓⾃2025年初建仓以
来已上涨约14.8倍。他认为Anthropic的IPO定价"便宜"，并给出了具体的估值逻辑。
信中写道："按我们的估算，Anthropic在2027年底年化经常性收⼊（ARR）上的市销率不⾜2
倍，在2027年底年化算⼒利润上的市盈率不⾜4倍。"他认为，考虑到该公司的增⻓速度与业务
质量，当前估值倍数明显偏低。
在盈利能⼒⽅⾯，信中指出，即便是Anthropic旗下轻量级模型Haiku，⽑利率也⾼达85%。
Aschenbrenner将AI实验室定性为"喷⽓发动机，⽽⾮航空公司"，认为Anthropic⾼度盈利，且递

归⾃我改进（RSI）技术将进⼀步拓宽其护城河。
SpaceX算⼒租赁协议：规模庞⼤，定价已⼤幅上调
信中披露了Anthropic与SpaceX之间⼀笔引⼈关注的算⼒租赁交易，并提供了具体定价细节。
据信函内容，今年5⽉，SpaceX向Anthropic租赁了约32.5万块GPU，分布于其Colossus园区，
⽉租⾦约12.5亿美元；6⽉，SpaceX⼜向⾕歌租赁约11万块GPU，⽉租⾦约9.2亿美元。两份协
议名义上延续⾄2029年，但双⽅均可在90天通知后提前终⽌，实质上接近于即期算⼒采购。
在定价层⾯，Situational Awareness估算，Anthropic协议最初以约3.50美元/千⽡时的价格签
订，⾕歌协议约为5.50美元/千⽡时。今年10⽉，Anthropic协议重新定价⾄约5.50美元/千⽡时，
涨幅约60%——尽管该协议所依赖的算⼒集群以效率相对较低的Hopper架构为主。信中还指
出，SpaceX可在不到⼀年内收回其集群投资成本，⽽这些集群的使⽤寿命超过五年。

AI进展提速，实验室收⼊或于2027年突破万亿

1 条评论
在宏观判断上，Aschenbrenner对AI技术进展的加速持有强烈信念，并据此对整个⾏业的收⼊规
模作出⼤胆预测。
信中称，AI进展速度⽬前已达到此前节奏的1.5⾄2倍，预计2027年⼀年内将实现相当于过去约
三年的技术进步量。基于这⼀判断，他预计各⼤AI实验室将在2027年底前达到超过1万亿美元的
年化收⼊规模。
在硬件定价⽅⾯，信中预计随着RSI技术落地，H100 GPU的即期定价将升⾄每⼩时数百美元。
在机器⼈领域，Aschenbrenner预计2027年底前后将出现类似ChatGPT的爆发时刻，并于2028
⾄2029年间进⼊"物理RSI"阶段。
市场定价保守，政策⻛险构成最⼤尾部威胁
尽管对AI前景⾼度乐观，Aschenbrenner也在信中对当前市场定价和潜在⻛险作出评估。

他认为，⽬前市场对AI进展的定价仅相当于0.3⾄0.6倍的"AI进展年"，存储芯⽚⼚商的估值仅反
映明年盈利的⼀倍市盈率，数据中⼼的估值也仅对应已签合同的价值，对冲基⾦整体已撤出该
赛道。这⼀判断构成其"现在是买⼊最佳时机"论断的核⼼依据。
在⻛险端，信中明确指出，政府对AI模型的监管封锁是最⼤的尾部⻛险，⼀旦发⽣，相关股票
可能⾯临80%的最⼤回撤。这也是Aschenbrenner在整体看多的框架下，少数明确量化的下⾏情
景之⼀。

利差優勢消長，新興與商品
貨幣走勢各異
熱門議題 / 投資關鍵
高油價、美元偏強及全球景氣分化持續牽動新興與
商品貨幣。澳洲就業轉弱、通膨改善，澳洲央行升
息週期料暫告一段落，澳幣短線料區間盤整。紐西
蘭央行意外按兵不動削弱紐幣短期動能，但升息預
期仍提供支撐。加拿大央行政策轉鷹及高油價利好
加幣，惟經濟與貿易不確定性限制升值空間。中國
內需偏弱，但出口暢旺、企業結匯需求強，加上政
策對升值容忍度提高，人民幣料溫和升值。南非幣
受美元、高油價及利差收窄壓抑，但財政紀律改善、
信用前景轉佳及實質利率優勢有助緩和貶值壓力，
料維持區間震盪。

《投資關鍵 – 澳洲景氣放緩、澳央轉向觀望，預期
澳幣盤整》
隨著國際油價維持高位，澳洲央行（RBA）於9月再
度升息將現金利率提高至4.6%，官員雖表示若通膨
壓力持續，不排除進一步緊縮。
但隨著高利率與高通膨壓力持續拖累經濟動能，近期
公布的9月就業意外減少3.93萬人、失業率更升至
4.7%，已連續兩個月落在澳央主席認為可有效壓抑
通膨的失業率4.5~5.0%區間；通膨方面，9月份澳洲
CPI雖由4.0%升至4.3%，但核心CPI僅有2.2%，加以
住房通膨明顯回落，因此本行預期澳央轉向觀望、本
輪升息暫時告終，2027年政策轉向觀望，如國際油
價回落至90美元以下，澳央有降息空間。
外需方面，中國經濟基本面持續低迷，消費與投資不

振亦難以挹注澳洲出口動能，外需對澳幣支撐力量有
限，加以預期聯準會在12月再度升息，將呈現「利
差有撐但優勢遞減」格局，因此預估澳幣短線維持在
0.7~0.71區間盤整，要進一步突破或要待2Q27美國
通膨回落、降息預期再度升溫，方有走揚力量。

紐西蘭通膨升溫訊號浮現，月度物價年增 5.2%
《投資關鍵 - 央行意外按兵不動拖累紐元，後續升息
預期提供下檔支撐》
過去一個月，受美元強勢、美債殖利率及油價走高拖
累，紐幣兌美元一度下探0.5565，其後隨升息預期升
溫而回穩。惟紐西蘭央行10月28日意外維持OCR於
2.75%不變，促使市場下修短期升息預期，紐幣再度
承壓。
紐西蘭經濟延續溫和復甦，企業信心及農產品價格改
善，惟勞動市場仍有閒置產能。雖然整體通膨低於預
期，但核心通膨及價格廣度上升，加上油價高企與紐

幣偏弱，我們預期4Q26通膨仍具黏性，價格壓力擴散
風險猶存。
我們仍維持偏鷹派的政策基準情境。10月以4比3票按
兵不動，較反映升息時點後移，而非整體政策路徑轉
向，緊縮週期尚未結束。我們仍預期12月及明年3月具
升息空間，尤其若通膨、油價及匯率壓力持續，政策
立場料維持偏緊。
展望4Q26，10月意外按兵不動削弱紐幣利差支撐，我
們仍看好紐元中期修復空間，惟短期上行動能有所減
弱。升息預期降溫，加上大選、美元及全球風險情緒
等不確定性，料使匯價維持較高波動。進入1Q27後，
隨政策不確定性逐步下降，經濟溫和復甦有望成為紐
幣主要支撐。考量匯價已反映相當程度的成長疑慮及
外部利空，若基本面持續改善，紐幣中期仍具修復空
間。

能源價格高企令加拿大通膨面臨上行風險，加拿大央

自10月中旬低點反彈。不過，隨本次25個基點升息
已大致反映於市場定價，加幣能否延續升勢將更取決
於央行對後續緊縮路徑的指引，以及市場對終端利率
的重新定價，而非本次升息本身。

首次升息，並表示若能源價格進一步推升廣泛通膨壓

測由2.0%上調至3.1%，並將今年經濟成長預測由
0.7%上調至1.0%，短期政策訊號偏鷹。惟核心通膨
仍相對溫和，勞動市場、製造業需求及招聘意願亦呈
轉弱跡象，意味若經濟活動進一步降溫，持續升息空
間或有限。高油價及偏高的投機性加幣空頭部位仍構
成短期利好，但美加貿易不確定性可能繼續拖累企業
投資及成長前景。因此，我們維持4Q26至1Q27加幣
中性看法。短期加美利差收窄及油價高企有望限制加

幣下行風險，但在增長前景改善或央行釋出更明確的
持續緊縮訊號前，加幣進一步升值空間有限。

，6月降息的可能性加大重點摘要
• 從景氣趨勢、政策取向與國
際經貿局勢變化三大視角切
入，綜合判斷人民幣溫和升

《投資關鍵 – 政經拉鋸與景氣K型下，人民幣溫和升值》
中國公布最新3Q26 GDP成長4.5%，出口年增率升至
28.4%，貿易順差由前季的3148.6億美元擴大至3651.2
億美元。市場預估中國2026 年全年貿易順差將超越
2025年1.2兆美元紀錄，再創新高。惟中國外需繁榮卻
無法掩蓋內需疲軟困境，中國3Q26零售銷售年增率僅
0.7%，固定資產投資衰退7%。儘管中國陸續推出穩增
長政策，大多以財政政策為主軸，貨幣政策偏向定向式
而非全面降準降息。因此儘管美啟動升息，中美利差尚
保持穩定，因此內需疲軟與有限度的寬鬆貨幣政策對人
民幣走勢並未形成顯著壓力。
中國的出口暢旺除受惠科技週期貢獻外，強勁汽車出口
至歐洲也是主因，9月中國汽車出口維持62%的高成長。
惟加劇保護主義傾向，中歐達成初步協議，未來四年內
將對歐洲的混合動力車出口量減少一半以上，具體配額、

最低售價及豁免條件仍待協商。政策角度觀察，人民幣
升值本身可能成為中國應對外部壓力的重要工具，減緩
低估匯率促進出口的國際批評。
考量關稅可能成為攻防戰手段之一，研判中國廠商或搶
出口支持外貿表現高檔，同時官方可能對人民幣升值空
間容忍態度提高，疊加前三季結售匯順差高達3,941億
美元，創2010年以來新高，顯示企業結匯意願強勁，持
續為人民幣提供支撐。從景氣趨勢、政策取向與國際經
貿局勢變化的三大視角切入，綜合判斷人民幣溫和升值
的看法不變。

### Page 6

《投資關鍵 - 基本面改善但部位趨擁擠，南非幣料維
持震盪》
南非幣第四季以來震盪偏弱，USD/ZAR近期回升至約
16.60。外部環境仍偏不利，高油價推升進口成本與
通膨壓力，加上美元偏強及美國南非利差收窄，削弱
南非幣套息吸引力。與此同時，巴西總統大選結果引
發市場波動，增添新興市場不確定性，短期投資人風
險偏好轉趨審慎，亦可能加重新興市場貨幣的資金流
出壓力。
基本面方面，南非財政紀律改善、主權信用前景轉佳，
加上較高實質利率及具吸引力的利差，仍為南非幣提
供支撐。惟高油價及聖嬰現象相關極端天氣風險令通
膨壓力偏高，限制政策寬鬆空間。另一方面，近期投
機性及資產管理機構多頭部位明顯增加，顯示市場部
位漸趨擁擠。在外部風險升溫及風險偏好轉弱下，獲
利了結與部位調整可能放大短期波動，即使匯價回檔，
逢低布局的風險報酬吸引力仍相對有限。
整體而言，南非幣基本面有所改善，但短期外部環境
與部位面均偏不利。財政紀律改善、主權信用前景轉
佳及實質利率優勢有助建立匯率下檔支撐，惟強勢美
元、高油價、利差收窄及偏擁擠的多頭部位限制升值

空間。預期4Q26美元兌南非幣仍以區間震盪為主，維
持南非幣中性看法。
重點摘要

2026 年 11 月 27 日 全 4 頁

中国：急発展する再生可能エネルギーと内巻 
太陽光・風力発電量を 25 年の 2.3 兆 kWh→30 年に 4 兆 kWh に拡大へ

調査本部 主席研究員 齋藤 尚登

[要約] 
 中国の太陽光発電と風力発電の発展が著しい。発電設備容量について、太陽光の構成
比は 2010 年の 0.0％から 2025 年は 30.8％に、同様に、風力発電は 3.1％から 16.4％
へと急拡大した。2010 年に 73.4％を占めていた火力発電のウエイトは徐々に低下し、
2025 年には 39.6％となった。一方、発電量の構成比は異なる様相を呈する。太陽光は
2010 年の 0.0％から 2025 年に 11.1％へ、風力は同様に 1.1％から 10.7％へと大きく拡
大したが、火力の 59.8％（2025 年）には遠く及ばない。太陽光発電や風力発電は気象
条件の影響を受け、稼働時間に制約があることがその背景だ。 
 2026 年 7 月に発表された「再生可能エネルギーの発展に関する第 15 次 5 カ年計画」で
は、2030 年までに再生可能エネルギーの発電設備容量を 35 億 kW（2025 年は 23.4 億
kW）へ拡大し、このうち太陽光発電と風力発電の設備容量を 28 億 kW（同 18.4 億 kW）
以上とし、非再生可能エネルギーを含む全体の発電設備容量に占める割合を 50％（同
47.3％）超に引き上げる、ことなどが目標に掲げられた。野心的な目標設定にみえる
が、これまでの爆発的な増加ぶりからすると、十分に達成可能であると思われる。 
 太陽光発電と風力発電はともに、過剰生産能力に伴う熾烈な価格競争に直面している。
いわゆる「内巻」の問題だ。結局のところ、問題の根源は過剰生産能力が増大する中
で、その削減が難しいことにある。国家の産業政策が奏功し、国有・民営企業を問わ
ず傾斜投資が行われ、国有商業銀行や地方の商業銀行が融資面で強力なサポートを提
供する。中央から見れば淘汰されてしかるべき企業・設備でも、地方にとっては保護
すべき対象ということになる。内巻の是正について、「再生可能エネルギーの発展に関
する第 15 次 5 カ年計画」でも抜本的な対策は盛り込まれていない。

太陽光発電・風力発電の発電設備容量が爆発的に増加 
中国の太陽光発電と風力発電の発展が著しい。発電設備容量を電源別に見たものが図表 1 で
あり、下段で構成比の推移を示している。これによると、太陽光発電は 2010 年の 0.0％から
2025 年は 30.8％に、同様に風力発電は 3.1％から 16.4％へと急拡大した。2010 年当時、73.4％
を占めていた火力発電のウエイトは徐々に低下し、2025 年には 39.6％となった。ちなみに、太
陽光発電と風力発電を合計すると、2025 年に初めて火力発電を上回り、2026 年 1 月～9 月は
10.2％pt の差を付けた。再生可能エネルギー（ここでは太陽光、風力、水力）の発電設備容量
は、2023 年に過半となり、2026 年 9 月には 6 割弱に達している。 
太陽光発電や風力発電の発電設備容量の爆発的な増加は、国家の産業政策の奏功によるとこ
ろが大きい。 
太陽光発電について、2009 年に中国財政部、科学技術部、国家エネルギー局が合同で「金太
陽パイロットプロジェクト」を実施したのを契機に、太陽光発電の導入が国家レベルで推進さ
れた。その後、2013 年に国務院が発表した「太陽光発電産業の健全な発展を促進することに関

3 / 4

する若干の意見」で、太陽光で発電された電力の固定価格買取制度が導入されたことが、太陽
光発電の発展に拍車をかけた。同制度は、地域ごとの日照条件に応じて、火力発電よりも高い
買取価格を設定して、太陽光発電のインセンティブとしたのである。このほか、太陽光発電関
連企業（ハイテク企業）に対する法人所得税の減免や各種補助金、銀行による低利融資の実施
などが、太陽光発電産業の勃興を支えた。

発電量の約 6 割は依然として火力発電。太陽光・風力は気象条件の影響が大 
次に、電源別の発電量を見たものが図表 2 であり、その構成比は、上記とは異なる様相を呈
している。太陽光発電の構成比は 2010 年の 0.0％から 2025 年に 11.1％へ、風力発電は同様に
1.1％から 10.7％へと大きく拡大しているが、火力発電の 59.8％（2025 年）には遠く及ばない。
太陽光発電や風力発電は気象条件の影響を受け、稼働時間に制約がある。当面の間、火力発電
が中国の主要電源である状況が続くとみられる。

再生可能エネルギーの発展に関する第 15 次 5 カ年計画 
中国国家発展改革委員会と国家エネルギー局は 2026 年 7 月に「再生可能エネルギーの発展に
関する第 15 次 5 カ年計画」を発表した。主な数値目標は以下のとおりだ。 
① 2030 年までに再生可能エネルギー発電設備容量を 35 億 kW（2025 年は 23.4 億 kW）へ拡大
する。このうち、太陽光発電と風力発電の設備容量を 28 億 kW（同 18.4 億 kW）以上とし、
全体の発電設備容量に占める割合を 50％（同 47.3％）超に引き上げる。 
② 再生可能エネルギーによる発電量を 6 兆 kWh（2025 年は 4 兆 kWh）へ拡大する。このうち、
太陽光発電と風力発電による発電量を 4 兆 kWh（同 2.3 兆 kWh）以上とし、全体の発電量に
占める割合を 30％（同 21.8％）へ引き上げる。

野心的な目標設定に見えるが、これまでの爆発的な増加ぶりからすると、十分に達成可能で
あると思われる。例えば、上記①に関連して、2020 年から 2025 年の間に、再生可能エネルギ
ー発電設備容量は 9.1 億 kW→23.4 億 kW に、うち太陽光発電と風力発電の設備容量は 5.4 億 kW
→18.4 億 kW に増加した。それぞれ 14.3 億 kW、13.0 億 kW の純増だ。2030 年までの 5 年間で必
要とされる純増分はこれを下回っている。

最大の課題は「内巻」の是正だが、前途多難 
太陽光発電と風力発電はともに、過剰生産能力に伴う熾烈な価格競争に直面している。いわ
ゆる「内巻」の問題だ。中国国内の太陽光発電関連銘柄は 98 社を数えるが、2025 年度決算で
は、そのうち 4 割弱が赤字決算を余儀なくされた。風力発電についても 2021 年～2024 年にか
けて風車メーカーの新規参入が相次ぎ、過剰生産能力の問題が深刻化した。陸上風車の単価が
大きく下がり、多くの企業の利益が圧迫されたのである。ただし、風力発電については、2024
年 10 月に主要風力発電設備メーカー12 社が原価割れでの入札の回避などの自主ルールを設け、
それが機能しているとされている。 
結局のところ、問題の根源は過剰生産能力が増大する中で、その削減が難しいことにある。
国家の産業政策が奏功し、国有・民営企業を問わず傾斜投資が行われ、国有商業銀行や地方の
商業銀行が融資面で強力なサポートを提供する。中央から見れば淘汰されてしかるべき企業・
設備でも、地方にとっては保護すべき対象ということになる。風力発電設備メーカーの自主ル
ールについては、協調が過度になれば、自由な競争が失われることになろう。内巻の是正につ
いて、「再生可能エネルギーの発展に関する第 15 次 5 カ年計画」でも抜本的な対策は盛り込ま
れていない。

Document 541: 住宅価格も金利も上昇、住宅ローン減税はどこまで効くか ～控除率引き上げという選択肢を考える～

Source PDF: all report pdf/住宅価格も金利も上昇、住宅ローン減税はどこまで効くか ～控除率引き上げという選択肢を考える～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-10
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

Research Report

2026 年11 月 10 日

1/7 
住宅価格も金利も上昇、住宅ローン減税はどこまで効くか 
～控除率引き上げという選択肢を考える～ 
 資産運用調査部 フェロー  谷口 智明

（要旨） 
○ 住宅価格の高騰と住宅ローン金利の上昇が重なり、住宅取得の負担が増している。本稿では、
住宅ローン減税の控除率と借入限度額（控除対象となる年末ローン残高の上限額）に着目し、
現行制度の負担軽減効果を確認した上で、今後の住宅取得支援の在り方を考察する。 
○ 住宅ローン減税は、借入限度額までの年末ローン残高に控除率を掛けた額を、所得税等から
差し引く仕組みだ。試算では、借入限度額を 4,500 万円とし、ローン残高は年間を通じて一
定、計算上の控除額は所得税等から全額差し引けると仮定する。控除率 0.7％、借入額4,000
万円では、金利が 0.5％から 1.2％へ上昇すると、利息に占める減税額の割合は 140％から約
58％へ低下する。金利 1.2％で借入額が 6,000 万円に増えると、借入限度額の制約もあり約
44％となる。控除率を 1.0％へ引き上げれば、それぞれ約83％、約63％へ高まる。なお、納
税額の制約で控除を使い切れない世帯では、負担軽減効果は小さくなる。 
○ 住宅ローン減税の控除率は、2022 年度税制改正で 1％から 0.7％に引き下げられた。これは、
低金利下で控除額が支払利息を上回る「逆ざや」への懸念等を背景とする。0.7％は当時の借
入金利等を踏まえて設定された水準であり、金利環境が変化した現在、逆ざやの抑制と住宅
取得支援のバランスを再検討することには合理性がある。 
○ 国土交通省の調査では、住宅ローン減税の利用者等の 21％が、制度がなければ住宅を購入し
なかったと回答した。購入判断等への影響が示唆される一方、2024 年度の税収減は約 1 兆円
に上る。また、住宅投資額の押上効果に関する既存の検証成果は制度の意義を考える重要な
蓄積だが、環境変化に応じて検証を継続し、制度の評価に生かす必要がある。 
○ 住宅価格と金利の同時上昇への対応策として、例えば、既存住宅の取得者も含む新規入居者
を対象に、取得当初の一定期間、控除率を 0.7％から 1.0％へ引き上げることが考えられる。
借入限度額の引き上げでは減税額が増えない家計にも、支援を厚くする手段となる。また、
納税額の制約で減税額が増えない世帯への対応として、取得時の補助等による支援も検討課
題といえる。住宅取得への効果と財政負担を継続的に見極めながら、住宅価格や金利環境の
変化に即して支援の対象や水準を見直し、制度の実効性を高めていくことが求められよう。

### Page 2

2/7 
1．住宅購入に重なる「価格高騰」と「金利上昇」 
住宅を購入しようとする家計は、住宅価格の高騰と住宅ローン金利の上昇という二つの変化に
直面している。国土交通省の不動産価格指数（2010 年平均＝100）によれば、2025 年12 月の全国
の住宅総合は 148.0、マンション（区分所有）は 225.1 となった（資料 1）。

資料 1 不動産価格指数（全国・住宅）の推移 
（注）季節調整値。2025 年 10～12 月は速報値。 
（出所）国土交通省「不動産価格指数」をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

地域や住宅の種類によって差はあるものの、とりわけマンション価格の上昇が際立つ。例え
ば、後述する住宅ローン減税（住宅借入金等特別控除）の控除率が 0.7％に引き下げられた 2022
年の初めから 2025 年末までに、マンション価格は約 3 割上昇している。 
住宅購入に必要な資金も大きい。国土交通省「令和 7 年度住宅市場動向調査」によれば、購入
資金の平均は土地取得を伴う注文住宅で7,646 万円、分譲集合住宅（分譲マンション等）で6,443
万円に上り、住宅取得に多額の資金を要することがうかがえる（注 1）。 
これに金利上昇が重なる。日本銀行は 2024 年3 月にマイナス金利政策を解除し、その後の段階
的な利上げを経て、2026 年 9 月には政策金利を1.25％程度まで引き上げた。新規借入向けの変動
金利は、大手銀行の公表例では 1％台に達している。住宅価格上昇で必要な借入額が膨らめば、金
利上昇による負担も一段と重くなる。 
こうした環境の下で、住宅ローン減税は住宅取得を目指す家計の負担をどこまで和らげるの
か。本稿では、控除率と借入限度額（控除対象となる年末ローン残高の上限額）に着目し、現行
制度の負担軽減効果を確認する。その上で、制度見直しの経緯や住宅取得への影響を踏まえ、今
後の支援の在り方を考察する。

2．住宅ローン減税の負担軽減効果をどう捉えるか 
住宅ローン減税は、一定の要件の下で年末ローン残高の 0.7％を所得税等から税額控除する仕組
80
100

140
160
180
200

戸建住宅
マンション（区分所有）

（2010年平均=100）

### Page 3

3/7 
みである。2026 年度税制改正では適用対象となる入居期限が 2030 年まで延長された。例えば、
2026 年入居で新築の長期優良住宅・低炭素住宅を取得した一般世帯の場合、借入限度額は 4,500
万円、控除期間は 13 年間である。子育て世帯や若者夫婦世帯には借入限度額の上乗せがあり、前
述の場合、借入限度額は 5,000 万円となる（注 2）。 
控除率と借入限度額は、金利や借入額の変化に対する負担軽減効果を左右する。借入限度額は
控除を受ける個人ごとに適用され、住宅の区分等によって異なる。ここでは、1 人で住宅ローンを
借り入れて新築の長期優良住宅・低炭素住宅を取得し、子育て世帯等への上乗せを適用しない場
合を想定する。金利・借入額別の単年の負担軽減効果について、控除率0.7％と 1.0％の場合で比
較した（資料 2）。

資料 2 借入額・金利・控除率別にみた利息と住宅ローン減税額（単年の機械的試算） 
（注）借入初年で、ローン残高は年間を通じて一定と仮定した機械的試算。年間利息＝ローン残高×金利、最大減税額＝min（ロ 
ーン残高、4,500 万円）×控除率（0.7％または 1.0％）。2026 年に新築の認定住宅へ入居し、1 人で借り入れる場合を想定。
子育て世帯・若者夫婦世帯に係る借入限度額の上乗せは適用せず、税額控除を満額利用できると仮定。ペアローン等によ
り複数人が控除を受ける場合、世帯全体の減税額は異なる。元金返済、手数料等は考慮していない。「利息－減税額」は住
宅取得に伴う総費用や実際の返済額を示すものではない。 
（出所）国土交通省「令和 8 年度税制改正要望結果等」をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

控除率0.7％、借入額 4,000 万円の場合、金利が0.5％から 1.2％へ上昇すると、年間利息は 20
万円から48 万円へ増える。最大減税額は 28 万円で変わらず、年間利息に占める減税額の割合は
140％から約58％へ低下する。同じ金利1.2％で借入額が6,000 万円に増えると、年間利息は 72
万円となる。借入限度額 4,500 万円を超える部分には控除が及ばず、最大減税額は 31.5 万円にと
どまるため、年間利息に占める減税額の割合は約 44％へ低下する。 
控除率を1.0％へ引き上げれば、同じ借入額・金利でも減税額が増えることから、年間利息に占
める割合は高まる。 
なお、納税額の制約で控除額を使い切れない場合、負担軽減効果はさらに小さくなる。

3．なぜ控除率は 1％から 0.7％に引き下げられたのか 
住宅ローン減税の控除率は 2022 年度税制改正（原則として 2022 年入居分から適用）で1％から
0.7％に引き下げられた。背景の一つは、資料2 でみたように、超低金利下で控除額が支払利息を
上回る「逆ざや」である。会計検査院は、2017 年に控除の適用を開始した調査対象 1,748 人の
（単位：万円）

（住宅ローン残高） 金利 年間利息 ① 最大減税額 ② 利息－減税額 （①－②） 減税額／利息 （②/①）

### Page 4

4/7 
78.1％が金利1％未満で借りていたことを指摘し、不要な借入や繰上返済の先送りを促すおそれを
問題視した（注3）。 
2022 年2 月 15 日の衆議院財務金融委員会で、政府は控除率を 0.7％とした理由について、金利
0.7％以上の適用者が全体の「6 割強」を占めていたことを挙げた。また、借入に伴う事務手数料
や保証料も加味し、不要な借入を促しにくい水準として 0.7％を設定したと説明した。さらに、実
際の支払利息を控除上限とする方法は制度が複雑になるため採用しなかったとも答弁している
（注 4）。つまり0.7％は、当時の借入金利や事務手数料・保証料等の借入費用と、制度の簡素性
を踏まえた水準であったといえる。 
それでは、近年の借入金利はどのように変化したのだろうか（資料 3）。

資料 3 借入金利の分布 
（注）インターネット調査。2026 年 1 月調査は、2025 年 4～9 月に住宅ローンを借りた全国の 20 歳以上 70 歳未満の人（学生・無
職を除く、1,237 人）が対象。「年 1.5％超」は、元資料の 4 区分を合算。端数処理により合計が 100％にならない場合がある。 
（出所）住宅金融支援機構「住宅ローン利用者調査（2026 年 1 月調査）」をもとに第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

控除率が引き下げられた時期に近い 2022 年 10 月調査と 2026 年 1 月調査を比べると、「年 0.5％
以下」は減少する一方、「年 0.5％超～1.0％以下」および「年1.0％超～1.5％以下」は大きく増
加している。ただし、「年 1.5％超」は減少していることに加え、金利区分は 0.7％を跨ぐため、
金利 0.7％以上の利用者の割合は把握できない。なお、2026 年 1 月調査は 2025 年4～9 月の借入
を対象としており、その後の金利上昇は反映されていないため、足元では、より高い金利で借り
入れた人の割合が増えていると推察される。また、既存の変動金利利用者も、適用金利の見直し
を通じて、利息負担が増えている可能性がある。 
住宅価格と金利が上昇するなか、控除率0.7％の設定時とは環境が変化している。控除率の水準
については、借入金利の分布や事務手数料・保証料等を含む借入費用の実態、税収減などを踏ま
え、改めて検討する意義があろう。

### Page 5

5/7 
4．減税は住宅取得を後押ししているか～約 1 兆円の税収減との関係 
国土交通省「住宅税制の活用状況等に関する意識調査（2025 年）」 では、2022～2025 年の住宅
取得・入居者（予定者を含む）のうち、住宅ローン減税の利用者および利用予定者に、住宅ロー
ン減税がなかった場合の影響について尋ねたところ、「購入しなかった」が 21％、「住宅購入や新
生活に影響が生じた」が 59％、「影響はなかった」が 20％だった。「影響が生じた」と回答した人
には、購入価格や住宅性能を下げる、家具・家電、生活費・教育費等を減らすといった回答がみ
られた。こうした結果は、住宅ローン減税が、購入判断だけでなく、住宅の選択や購入後の暮ら
しにも影響を与えている可能性を示している。 
住宅ローン減税には相応の財政負担も伴う。国土交通省（2025）によれば、2024 年度の税収減
（見込額を含む）は国税 8,280 億円、地方税1,790 億円の計 1 兆 70 億円に上る。同制度がない場
合に比べた住宅投資額の押上効果は、分析方法や仮定の異なる複数の試算で約 0.9～5.8 兆円と推
計されている。同省は、データの制約等から他の支援措置の影響を切り分けることには限界があ
るものの、こうした推計やアンケート結果を踏まえ、住宅取得の促進や内需拡大等に一定の効果
があると評価している。 
これらの検証成果は、制度の意義を考える上で重要な蓄積である。ただし、住宅投資額の増加
は、そのまま税収増を意味するわけではない。財政負担との関係を捉える際には、各試算の前提
や対象とする効果の範囲に留意し、検証成果を制度の評価に生かす必要がある。

5．住宅価格と金利の同時上昇にどう応えるか 
住宅価格と金利の同時上昇により、住宅取得の負担が増している。住宅ローン減税の控除率
0.7％は低金利下の逆ざやを抑える観点等から見直されたが、住宅価格や金利環境が変化した現
在、逆ざやの抑制と住宅取得支援のバランスについて、改めて検討する余地がある。 
例えば、負担増への対応策として、控除率の水準や適用期間は慎重に見極める必要があるもの
の、既存住宅の取得者も含む新規入居者を対象に、取得当初の一定期間、控除率を0.7％から
1.0％へ引き上げることなどが考えられる。資料 2 の試算が示すように、控除率の上乗せは、借入
限度額の引き上げだけでは減税額が増えない家計への支援を厚くする手段となる。返済残高が大
きい取得当初の負担を軽減することで、住宅取得を目指す家計の購入判断を後押しする効果も期
待できる。 
また、納税額の制約で控除率を上乗せしても減税額が増えない世帯への対応として、取得時の
補助等による支援も検討課題といえる。その際、所得等を踏まえた対象の設定や事務負担といっ
た制度設計上の諸課題を整理する必要があろう。 
住宅取得支援は供給増につながることもあるが、OECD（2022）は、供給を増やしにくい地域で
は支援の一部が住宅価格に転嫁され、購入者の負担軽減効果が弱まる可能性も指摘している（注
5）。地域の供給状況を踏まえ、既存住宅の流通・改修の促進等と組み合わせ、家計が取得可能な
住宅の選択肢を広げることも重要だ。 
住宅ローン減税とその効果検証を一体として捉えることも欠かせない。住宅取得への効果と財

### Page 6

6/7 
政負担を継続的に見極めながら、住宅価格や金利環境の変化に即して支援の対象や水準を見直
し、制度の実効性を高めていくことが求められよう。
